# Wave B Hindi re-map sheet

Generated by `apps/web/scripts/hindi-remap-sheet.mjs` from two pinned snapshots: the dictionaries at the base commit `af11810` (apps/web/scripts/fixtures/wave-b-base.json) and the dictionaries `apps/web/messages/en.json`, `hi.json` and `tools/*.json` as they stood at commit `6b5c15a` (apps/web/scripts/fixtures/wave-b-head.json). The counts and the last two sections come from a third pinned file, apps/web/scripts/fixtures/v1-hindi-remap.json, made by `apps/web/scripts/v1-hindi-remap.mjs` from Dr. Sharma's newest v1.

This sheet is a snapshot at that commit, not a live view: the dictionaries keep changing after it was generated. To refresh it, run `pnpm --filter @appuscholar/web i18n:remap` (it rewrites the head snapshot and then this sheet) and commit both files together. Do not edit by hand.

All Hindi in this sheet is DRAFT, for Dr. Sharma's review. Quoted English and Hindi are verbatim, including any emoji in the old strings. The one exception is the section "Hindi remapped from v1", which is her own Hindi.

- Keys at the base: 1,141
- Hindi values that were English copies at the base: 366
- Keys now: 1,716
- Keys changed since the base (English text differs): 40
- Keys added since the base: 579
- Keys removed since the base (not listed as rows): 4
- Keys whose only change is the emoji: 0
- English copies before the v1 remap, main dictionary: 369
- Remapped from v1 build 2026.10.27d (commit `6b70c3e`): 159 (exact English 147, same words 7, placeholders renamed 5)
- Remapped, by where v1 keeps the Hindi: I18N dictionary 114, stage table AP_STAGES_HI 23, toast table TOAST_HI 20, dialog table STATIC_HI 2
- Still English after the remap, main dictionary: 213 (no Hindi in v1 190, ambiguous in v1 1, v1 Hindi not used 8, a name or format 11, added after the remap 3)
- Hindi values still English now, main dictionary: 213
- Hindi values still English now, tool files: 17
- Hindi values still English now, both: 230
- Tool-file strings (all new since the base): 1,275
- Tool-file strings whose Hindi is an English copy: 17
- Tool-file strings with no Hindi yet (the app shows the English): 57

Statuses: "Hindi was an English copy" (the Hindi at the base was the English text; it needs translating); "Hindi re-drafted" (the English changed and the Hindi changed with it); "English changed, Hindi not updated" (the English changed and the Hindi is the old Hindi; it needs a check); "emoji only" (the only change is an emoji; no new Hindi needed); "new key" (added, with a draft Hindi); "new key, Hindi is still English" (added, Hindi not yet translated); "new key, no Hindi yet" (added to the English file only).

| `faq_a_lost` | `Your documents are saved under your account with every version, so clearing a browser does not lose them. Sign in on any device to find them. Sign in on any device to find them. If a document is missing, check the Doc list and Studies, then write to us.` | `आपके दस्तावेज़ हर संस्करण के साथ आपके खाते के अंतर्गत सहेजे जाते हैं, इसलिए ब्राउज़र साफ़ करने से वे नहीं खोते। किसी भी डिवाइस पर साइन इन करके उन्हें पाएँ। कोई दस्तावेज़ न दिखे तो दस्तावेज़ सूची और शोध प्रोजेक्ट्स देखें, फिर हमें लिखें।` | `आपके दस्तावेज़ हर संस्करण के साथ आपके खाते के अंतर्गत सहेजे जाते हैं, इसलिए ब्राउज़र साफ़ करने से वे नहीं खोते। किसी भी डिवाइस पर साइन इन करके उन्हें पाएँ। कोई दस्तावेज़ न दिखे तो दस्तावेज़ सूची और शोध अध्ययन देखें, फिर हमें लिखें।` | Hindi re-drafted |

| samples | `wsamp_desc` | `Appuscholar keeps a private copy of the words you typed yourself in each of your documents. It leaves out anything inserted from an AI suggestion, any document you marked as a source, and any document with fewer than 30 typed words. The copy is stored on the Appuscholar server with your study, not on this computer. With a vault it is locked with your passphrase, as your documents are; without one it is stored the way your documents are. No tool uses it yet. It is included when you export all your work, and a document's copy is removed when you delete that document or mark it as a source. Turn this off any time and clear what is saved; your documents are never affected either way.` | `Appuscholar आपके हर दस्तावेज़ में आपके खुद टाइप किए गए शब्दों की एक निजी प्रति रखता है। इसमें AI सुझाव से डाला गया हिस्सा, स्रोत के रूप में चिह्नित दस्तावेज़ और 30 से कम टाइप किए शब्दों वाले दस्तावेज़ शामिल नहीं होते। यह प्रति इस कंप्यूटर पर नहीं, आपके अध्ययन के साथ Appuscholar के सर्वर पर रखी जाती है। तिजोरी होने पर यह आपके दस्तावेज़ों की तरह आपके पासफ़्रेज़ से बंद रहती है; तिजोरी न होने पर यह उसी तरह रखी जाती है जैसे आपके दस्तावेज़। अभी कोई टूल इसका उपयोग नहीं करता। जब आप अपना सारा काम निर्यात करते हैं तब यह उसमें शामिल होती है, और किसी दस्तावेज़ को हटाने या स्रोत के रूप में चिह्नित करने पर उसकी प्रति हट जाती है। इसे कभी भी बंद करें और सहेजी हुई प्रतियां मिटाएं; आपके दस्तावेज़ किसी भी स्थिति में प्रभावित नहीं होते।` | new key |
| samples | `wsamp_full` | `This study's saved copy is full, so newer writing is not being added. Clear it to make room.` | `इस अध्ययन की सहेजी हुई प्रति भर चुकी है, इसलिए नया लेखन जोड़ा नहीं जा रहा। जगह बनाने के लिए इसे मिटाएं।` | new key |

| style | `sty2_passage_0` | `Many students believe that studying late into the night helps them remember more, but the evidence points the other way. Sleep gives the brain time to sort what it has learned and to store the important parts. A student who sleeps well before an examination usually recalls facts faster and makes fewer careless mistakes than a student who stayed awake to revise one more chapter.` | none | new key, no Hindi yet |
| style | `sty2_passage_1` | `A quiet library is often praised as the best place to study, yet some students work better with a little background noise. A café, a corridor or a shared room can hold attention for people who find silence tiring. The useful question is not which place is best for everyone, but which place lets a particular student keep working for an hour without drifting.` | none | new key, no Hindi yet |
| style | `sty2_passage_2` | `Group study can help when each member prepares alone first and then explains the topic to the others. Explaining forces a student to find the gaps in their own understanding. It helps much less when the group meets only to copy notes, because nobody then has to put an idea into their own words, and the sense of progress is greater than the progress itself.` | none | new key, no Hindi yet |

## Hindi remapped from v1: 159 keys

Until now Hindi mode showed the English text for these keys. They now show the Hindi in Dr. Sharma's own v1 (build 2026.10.27d, commit `6b70c3e`), found by the English text; v1's key is in the table (a key such as `TOAST_HI["..."]` or `AP_STAGES_HI.gap.name` names one of the tables beside v1's dictionary: toasts, dialog text, the stage names and questions of Analyse a paper). The Hindi is hers, not a draft, but it was written for v1's screens, so please check it in place. Three things were adjusted to keep the two languages alike: "Appuscholer" is spelt "Appuscholar", the Hindi has the same edge spaces as the English, and the Hindi starts with the same emoji as the English (or none).

| Key | English | Hindi from v1 | v1 key | Match |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ap2_abstract_only_shallow_an` | `Abstract only, shallow. An abstract is a summary of about 150 to 300 words. Most questions about method, sample, measures and limits cannot be answered from it, so expect many answers to say that the paper text does not report this. For a real analysis, get the full paper (the free PDF button on the search result, where one exists), download it and load it here.` | `केवल सार (abstract), इसलिए उथला। सार लगभग 150 से 300 शब्दों का सारांश होता है। method, sample, measures और सीमाओं के ज़्यादातर सवालों का जवाब इससे नहीं मिलता, इसलिए कई जवाब कहेंगे कि पाठ में यह नहीं बताया गया। असली विश्लेषण के लिए पूरा शोधपत्र लें (खोज परिणाम पर मुफ़्त PDF बटन, जहाँ हो), उसे डाउनलोड करें और यहाँ डालें।` | `ap_abs` | exact English |

## Hindi still English after the remap: 213 keys

Hindi mode shows the English text for these keys. Reasons: "no Hindi in v1" (v1 has no Hindi for that English, in its dictionary or in its toast, dialog and stage tables, often because v1 itself still shows it in English; Dr. Sharma to provide); "ambiguous in v1" (v1 has two different Hindi values for it; she is asked to choose); "v1 Hindi not used" (v1's Hindi has other tags, placeholders or emoji than the English); "a name or format" (a product name, a file format or a typed word, which stays as it is in both languages).

| Key | English | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `ai_pill_key` | `AI: {provider}` | added after the remap, not checked against v1 |
| `ai_pill_wait` | `AI: …` | added after the remap, not checked against v1 |
| `ap2_a_warning_signal_only` | `. A warning signal only; no flag is not proof of soundness.` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_across_csv` | `⬇ Download as CSV` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_ai_reading_check_it` | `(AI reading, check it)` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_also_show_the_ai` | `Also show the AI readings in separate columns (they can be wrong; your words stay first)` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_analyse_a_paper` | `📄 Analyse a paper` | ambiguous in v1: `tab_analyse` says 📄 शोध-पत्र का विश्लेषण, `ap_h3` says 📄 शोधपत्र का विश्लेषण |
| `ap2_cells_are_your_words` | `Cells are your words from the walk-through and the card. An empty cell means you have not written it yet. Cards live in this browser; back them up in Settings, Backup.` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_characters_read` | `characters read` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_check_the_numbers_each` | `<b>Check the numbers.</b> Each p-value below was recomputed from the printed statistic and its degrees of freedom, allowing for rounding.` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_check_the_numbers_no` | `<b>Check the numbers.</b> No test result in the form t(df) = value, p = value (or F, r, χ²) was found in the text. Many papers print results in tables, which this check cannot read.` | no Hindi in v1 |
| `ap2_check_yourself` | `<b>Check yourself.</b>` | v1 Hindi not used: tags differ (v1 key `ap_chk`) |
